L2674: Figure 1: PS0014 Schematic (Weeks 0-48) illustrates the study design for Cohort A and Cohort B, showing the Feeder Study Dose and Treatment period (open label).
Cohort A starts with a Feeder Study Dose (Weeks 0-16) and then enters the Treatment period (Weeks 16-48).
Placeboa (Bimekizumab 320mg Q4W Week 16 → Placebo): If PASI90 is achieved, subjects proceed to Bimekizumab 320mg Q4W.
Ustekinumab: If PASI90 is achieved, subjects proceed to Bimekizumab 320mg Q4W.
Bimekizumab 320mg Q4Wb: If PASI90 is achieved, subjects proceed to Bimekizumab 320mg Q8W.
Bimekizumab 320mg Q8W: If PASI90 is achieved, subjects proceed to Bimekizumab 320mg Q8W.
Cohort Bd starts with a Feeder Study Dose (Weeks 0-16) and then enters the Treatment period (Weeks 16-48).
Direct enrollees with PSO, GPP, and EP proceed to Bimekizumab 320mg Q4W.
The Treatment period (Weeks 16-48) includes dosing intervals (Q4W or Q8W) and a safety follow-up visit 20 weeks after the last dose.
L2675: Figure 2: PS0014 Schematic (Weeks 48-144)
L2676: Figure 3 is a schematic diagram illustrating the study design for Trial PS0015 (Weeks 0-144).
The study begins with a Screening period (2-5 weeks) followed by a Treatment period (double-blind) from Week 0 to Week 48.
At the start of the Treatment period, 700 subjects (N=700) were randomized 1:1 into two arms:
Bimekizumab 320mg Q4W (n=350): This arm continued treatment with Bimekizumab 320mg Q4W through Week 16. At Week 16, subjects in this arm were rerandomized 1:2 to either Bimekizumab 320mg Q4W or Bimekizumab 320mg Q8W (Weeks 16-48).
Secukinumab 300mg Q4W (n=350): This arm continued treatment with Secukinumab 300mg Q4W through Week 48.
Following Week 48, all subjects entered the Open-label extension period (Weeks 48-144).
The timeline shows dosing events (Dosing, Dosing (SEC or PBO)) occurring every 4 weeks starting from Baseline (Week 0) up to Week 44. The Primary endpoint, PASI100 response, is assessed at Week 16.
After the final dose at Week 48, subjects proceed to a Safety follow-up visit 20 weeks after last dose, or enter the Open-label extension period.
L2677: Figure 4: PS0015 Schematic (Weeks 48-144) illustrates the treatment schedule for the Open-label extension period, starting at Week 48.
The timeline shows Weeks 52 through 144, with visits at Weeks 52, 56, 60, 64, 68, 72, 76, 80, 84, 88, 92, 96, 100, 104, 108, 112, 116, 120, 124, 128, 132, 136, 140, and 144. A safety follow-up visit is scheduled 20 weeks after the last dose.
The treatment groups are defined by PASI90 status at Week 48:
<PASI90: Bimekizumab 320mg Q4W (top row) or Bimekizumab 320mg Q8W (middle row).
\gePASI90: Secukinumab 300mg Q4W (top row) or Secukinumab 300mg Q8W (middle row).
Participants in the Bimekizumab 320mg Q4W group (top row) are subject to a Default switch from Q4W to Q8W, indicated by dashed arrows pointing down to the Bimekizumab 320mg Q8W group (bottom row).
Participants in the Secukinumab 300mg Q4W group (top row) are subject to 1:1 re-randomization, indicated by dashed arrows pointing down to the Secukinumab 300mg Q8W group (middle row).
The bottom row shows the final dosing schedule for all participants, starting at Week 52. The schedule is Q4W dosing initially, followed by Q8W dosing. The legend indicates:
(Q4W) Q4W dosing is only applicable for participants not yet switched to Q8W dosing.
Q4W dosing will be skipped at this visit in order to switch participants to Q8W dosing, if not done previously.
On site visits where participants will switch to Q8W dosing. After Week 64 only, if not already done at a previous on site visit.

The plot displays simulated median response rates and 5th and 95th percentiles for three treatment groups: 320mg Q4W (green), 320mg Q8W (red), and IGA0/1 (yellow/orange). The lines are colored by the response variable type: PASI100 (purple dashed line) and PASI90 (blue solid line).
The 320mg Q4W group shows a response rate of approximately 10% across all C_{av} deciles. The 320mg Q8W group shows a response rate of approximately 5% across all C_{av} deciles. The IGA0/1 group shows a response rate increasing from approximately 50% at low C_{av} deciles to approximately 95% at high C_{av} deciles.

L2688: Figure 11 is a schematic diagram illustrating the Trial Design for PS0008. The trial consists of three parallel arms, each starting with a Screening phase followed by an Initial Treatment Period (double-blind) and a Maintenance Treatment Period. The total duration is 56 weeks, marked by Week 0 (Baseline), Week 16, Week 24, and Week 56.
The arms are:
Arm 1 (N=150): Initial Treatment Period: Bimekizumab 320mg Q4W. Maintenance Treatment Period: Bimekizumab 320mg Q4W.
Arm 2 (N=150): Initial Treatment Period: Bimekizumab 320mg Q4W. Maintenance Treatment Period: Bimekizumab 320mg Q8W.
Arm 3 (N=150): Initial Treatment Period: Adalimumab. Maintenance Treatment Period: Bimekizumab 320mg Q4W.
At Week 16, the Co-Primary Endpoint (PASI90 Response, IGA response) is assessed. Following the 56-week period, subjects in Arms 1 and 2 proceed to a Bimekizumab open-label study to evaluate response to treatment and long-term safety. Subjects in Arm 3 proceed to an SFU Visit (Safety Follow-Up) 20 weeks after the last dose for subjects not enrolling in the extension study.
L2689: Figure 12 is a schematic diagram illustrating the Trial Design for PS0009. The overall timeline is divided into three phases: Screening, Initial Treatment Period, and Maintenance Treatment Period.
The trial began with a total of N=560 subjects. These subjects were randomized into three arms:
Placebo (N=80)
Bimekizumab 320mg Q4W (N=320)
Ustekinumab (N=160)
The Initial Treatment Period lasted 16 weeks (from Baseline to Week 16). The Maintenance Treatment Period lasted 36 weeks (from Week 16 to Week 52).
At Week 16, the Co-primary Endpoint (PASI90 Response and IGA Response) was assessed.
At Week 52, subjects were eligible for enrollment in the Bimekizumab extension study to evaluate response to treatment and long-term safety. Subjects not enrolling in the extension study entered the SFU Visit (Safety Follow-Up Visit) 20 Weeks after last dose.
L2690: Figure 13 is a schematic diagram illustrating the Trial Design for PS0013. The trial timeline spans from Baseline (Week 0) to Week 56, divided into Screening, Initial Treatment Period (Weeks 0-16), Randomized-Withdrawal Period (Weeks 16-56), and Safety Follow-up (Weeks 56-76).
The trial starts with N=400 subjects entering the Initial Treatment Period (Weeks 0-16). Subjects are randomized 1:1:1 based on PASI90 response at Week 16:
Subjects achieving \gePASI90 (N=320) enter the Randomized-Withdrawal Period (Weeks 16-56). They are randomized 1:1:1 into three arms:Bimekizumab 320mg Q4W
Bimekizumab 320mg Q8W
Placebo Q4W

Subjects not achieving \gePASI90 (N=80) enter the Placebo arm (Weeks 16-56).
At Week 20, subjects in the Randomized-Withdrawal Period are assessed for relapse:
Subjects in the Bimekizumab arms who relapse (<PASI75 at Week 20 or later) enter the 12-week, open-label escape arm: Bimekizumab 320mg Q4W.
Subjects in the Placebo arm who relapse (<PASI75 at Week 20 or later) enter the 12-week, open-label escape arm: Bimekizumab 320mg Q4W.
Subjects who do not relapse (PASI75 or better at Week 20) continue their assigned arm until Week 56.
At Week 56, all subjects (including those in the escape arm) are eligible for the Bimekizumab extension study to evaluate response to treatment and long-term safety, or an SFU Visit (20 Weeks after last dose for subjects not enrolling in extension study).
Co-Primary Endpoint: PASI90 Response, IGA response.

L2696: Schematic Diagram of Trial PS0016 showing the study timeline and subject flow.
The study timeline is divided into Screening (-4 Weeks), Treatment Period (Baseline - Week 16, Week 20, Week 24, Week 28), and Safety Follow-up (Week 36).
Subjects (N=90) were randomized 2:1 into Treatment arm A (N=30) and Treatment arm B (N=15).
Treatment arm A: Bimekizumab 320 mg SC at Baseline and Week 4, and Placebo at Week 16.
Treatment arm B: Bimekizumab 320 mg SC at Baseline, Week 4, and Week 16.
Subjects in both arms were assessed for PASI25 response at Week 20, Week 24, and Week 28.
Subjects who were PASI25 responders at Week 20 (YES path) continued off-treatment assessment (Week 24, Week 28, Week 36 SFU Visit). Subjects who were PASI25 non-responders at Week 20 (NO path) were assessed at Week 24, Week 28, and Week 36 SFU Visit.
Subjects who were PASI25 responders in the previous 16 weeks (YES path) were eligible for the Extension Study (separate consent).
Subjects who were PASI25 non-responders in the previous 16 weeks (NO path) were assessed at Week 24, Week 28, and Week 36 SFU Visit.
Legend: Bimekizumab 320mg, Placebo.
* PASI25 responder at at least one of the following visits; Week 2, 4, 8, 12 or 16
